### [1] [Rendezvous of Mobile Deterministic Automata in Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06482)
*Bibhuti Das,Andrzej Pelc*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个移动智能体在图中相遇的问题，证明了即使使用任意数量的静止鹅卵石也无法在树上实现通用相遇，但使用单个可移动鹅卵石可以设计出针对树的通用相遇确定性有限自动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究是否存在一个通用的确定性有限自动机(DFA)，能够在所有可行的实例中保证两个相同的智能体在任意延迟情况下实现相遇。这是图论中相遇问题的核心挑战，具有广泛的应用价值。

Method: 首先观察无标记能力下的不可能性结果，然后引入鹅卵石机制。对比分析两种鹅卵石类型：静止鹅卵石(只能放下不能拾起)和可移动鹅卵石(可以放下和拾起)。针对树结构这一受限图类进行深入研究。

Result: 证明了两个关键结论：(1)对于任意有限数量的静止鹅卵石，不存在针对树的RV通用DFA；(2)设计了针对树的RV通用DFA，其中每个智能体配备单个可移动鹅卵石。这表明鹅卵石的可移动性是实现通用相遇的关键特征。

Conclusion: 鹅卵石的可移动性是解决树结构中通用相遇问题的关键因素。即使使用多个静止鹅卵石也无法实现，而单个可移动鹅卵石即可构造出有效的解决方案，揭示了移动能力在分布式相遇算法中的重要性。

Abstract: Two mobile agents, modeled as identical deterministic finite automata (DFA) navigating in synchronous rounds in a graph with unlabeled nodes, have to meet at some node. The well-researched task of meeting in a graph is known as rendezvous. Agents start at adversarially chosen distinct nodes in possibly different rounds. An instance of the rendezvous problem is the underlying graph, together with the initial nodes $u$ and $v$ of the agents. Such an instance is feasible, if there exists a DFA (possibly working only for this instance), such that its identical copies starting at nodes $u$ and $v$, with an arbitrary delay, accomplish rendezvous. A DFA is RV-universal for a class of instances, if it guarantees rendezvous of its copies starting at the designated nodes with arbitrary delay, for all feasible instances of this class. Our goal is to investigate the existence of RV-universal DFA.
  We start by observing that if agents cannot mark nodes in any way then there does not exist a RV-universal DFA even for the class of instances where the underlying graph is a line. Hence we allow the use of identical pebbles to mark the nodes by the agents. We consider stationary pebbles that can be dropped by agents at nodes but cannot be picked up, and movable pebbles that can be dropped by agents and later picked up. We observe that, even in the more powerful scenario of movable pebbles, if agents are equipped with any finite number of pebbles, there is no RV-universal DFA for the class of all instances. Hence we restrict attention to instances where the underlying graph is a tree. Our main contribution are two contrasting results showing that movability of pebbles is a crucial feature. We first prove that for any finite number of stationary pebbles there is no RV-universal DFA for trees, and then we design a RV-universal DFA for trees, where each agent is equipped with a single movable pebble.

</details>


### [2] [Cascade: Exploiting SLO-Aware latency budget for fair and high goodput LLM inference serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06557)
*Muhammad Adnan,Rohan Mahapatra,Prashant J. Nair,Daniel Berger,Pantea Zardoshti,Rodrigo Fonseca,Esha Choukse*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出Cascade系统，通过引入“per-request latency budget”概念，将请求调度与KV缓存管理统一协调，显著提升LLM服务的SLO满足率和系统吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务平台对同一服务内的请求采用统一的响应延迟SLO，但请求在输入长度、生成长度、执行成本和KV缓存状态等方面差异巨大，导致部分请求有充足的延迟余量而其他请求几乎没有。传统方法未能区分请求的紧急程度，无法有效平衡系统资源分配。

Method: Cascade系统基于请求特征、KV缓存状态和系统负载估算并持续更新每个请求的延迟预算（SLO与预测剩余服务时间的差值）。该预算同时指导：(1)调度器优先服务剩余预算不足的请求；(2)内存管理器根据预算决定非驻留KV状态的恢复/预取、HBM保留或重新计算。通过将排队和数据移动开销导向能承受的请求，实现调度与内存管理的联合优化。

Result: 在三种大语言模型的生产负载trace上，Cascade将SLO满足的goodput提升高达2.4倍，SLO违规率降低40%，同时保持了异构请求类别间的公平性。

Conclusion: 通过统一的per-request latency budget机制协同优化请求调度和KV缓存管理，Cascade有效提升了LLM服务系统的性能和资源利用效率，为差异化请求场景下的服务质量管理提供了新思路。

Abstract: The reasoning and agentic capabilities of large language models have expanded the range of applications they support, from short interactive exchanges to long, compute-heavy requests. LLM serving platforms today define response-latency service-level objectives, even though requests within the same service can differ by orders of magnitude in input length, generation length, execution cost, and the availability of reusable KV-cache state. As a result, requests governed by the same service level objective have different urgency: after accounting for the time required to execute them, some have substantial latency headroom while others have almost none. We define this headroom---the difference between a request's service level objective and its predicted remaining service time---as its per-request latency budget.
  We present Cascade, an LLM serving system that estimates and continuously updates this budget from request characteristics, KV-cache state, and current system load. Unlike prior SLO-aware schedulers that use deadlines to govern request ordering alone, Cascade uses a single per-request budget to jointly coordinate request scheduling and KV-cache management across the memory hierarchy. Its scheduler prioritizes requests with little remaining budget, while its memory manager uses the same budget to decide whether non-resident KV state should be restored or prefetched from a deeper tier, retained in HBM, or recomputed. By directing queueing and data-movement overhead toward requests that can absorb it, Cascade improves SLO-satisfied goodput while preserving fairness across heterogeneous request classes. On production traces across three large language models, Cascade improves goodput by up to2.4x and reduces SLO violations by 40% relative to the default vLLM first-come, first-served scheduler.

</details>


### [3] [MARS: A Monte Carlo Tree Search-based Adaptive and Responsive Scheduler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06629)
*Yash Kurkure,Yihe Zhang,Zhiling Lan,Michael E. Papka*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了MARS，一种无需训练的HPC调度器，通过蒙特卡洛树搜索和轻量级离散事件模拟器，在严格时间预算内探索调度决策的未来影响，支持通过奖励函数配置优化目标。在Argonne领导计算设施的Theta和Polaris系统上评估显示，MARS-CW将尾部等待时间分别减少64%和43%，MARS-CU有效恢复维护前的系统利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高性能计算系统依赖静态启发式方法和手动管理进行作业调度和预留管理。深度强化学习（DRL）虽展现出良好的调度性能，但需要历史训练数据且在训练时固定优化目标，导致操作员在优先级变化时必须重新训练模型。

Method: 提出MARS（基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的自适应响应调度器），采用无需训练的方法，通过奖励函数而非学习模型配置优化目标。MARS使用轻量级离散事件模拟器，在严格时间预算内探索调度决策的未来后果，在每个调度周期适应配置的奖励函数。通过前瞻性策略主动排空系统并围绕未来预留进行规划。

Result: 在Argonne领导计算设施的4,360节点Theta系统和560节点Polaris系统上，使用全年生产工作负载进行评估。在等待时间最小化目标（MARS-CW）下，相比生产WFP启发式方法，Theta系统尾部等待时间减少64%，Polaris系统减少43%。在利用率最大化目标（MARS-CU）下，成功恢复维护前48小时的系统利用率，证明了通过奖励重配置可以灵活针对不同优化目标。

Conclusion: MARS通过前瞻性调度有效避免了预留窗口前典型的碎片化和利用率下降问题，相比DRL和启发式方法的被动响应策略展现出显著优势。该方法实现了优化目标的灵活配置，无需重新训练即可适应不同的调度优先级，为HPC系统调度提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern High Performance Computing systems depend on static heuristics and manual administration for job scheduling and reservation management. Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has shown promising scheduling performance but requires historical training data and fixes the optimization goal at training time, forcing operators to retrain whenever priorities shift. We introduce MARS (Monte Carlo Tree Search-based Adaptive and Responsive Scheduler), a training-free HPC scheduler whose optimization goal is configurable through a reward function rather than baked into a learned model. MARS uses a lightweight discrete-event simulator to explore the future consequences of scheduling decisions within a strict time budget, adapting to the configured reward at each scheduling cycle. We evaluate MARS on year-long production workloads from two systems at Argonne Leadership Computing Facility -- 4,360-node Theta and 560-node Polaris---under two reward functions: wait-time minimization (MARS-CW) and utilization maximization (MARS-CU). Unlike DRL and heuristics, which only react to the current queue or wait for backfill to find holes, MARS exploits look-ahead to proactively drain the system and plan around future reservations, packing the system to avoid the fragmentation and utilization drop that typically precede reservation windows. MARS-CW reduces tail wait time by 64% on Theta and 43% on Polaris over the production WFP heuristic, while MARS-CU recovers utilization in the 48 hours leading into maintenance, demonstrating that MARS can target either objective via reward reconfiguration.

</details>


### [4] [DGEMM with Ozaki Scheme I/II on FP4 Tensor Cores: A Base-13 E2M1 Limb Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06812)
*Shun-ichiro Hayashi,Daichi Mukunoki,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出在FP4 Tensor Core上实现Ozaki方案I和II，以模拟FP64矩阵乘法，通过将FP4值转换为基13整数表示，在FP32累加器中实现无误差中间求和，性能可与现有FP8实现相媲美甚至超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于INT8和FP8的Ozaki方案实现已经存在，但更快的FP4 Tensor Core尚未被利用来实现高精度矩阵乘法。由于现代GPU（如Blackwell架构）提供FP4 Tensor Core，其吞吐量是FP8的两倍，因此探索在FP4上实现高精度计算具有重要意义。

Method: 利用FP4数值的一个关键特性：每个FP4值在加倍后都变成整数，且通过13的倍数移位可以覆盖所有整数。基于此特性，将任意整数转换为基13的FP4表示（limbs），使得在FP32累加器中的中间求和保持无误差，从而实现Ozaki方案I和II。同时，该方法也可在FP4 Tensor Core上比特精确地模拟INT8 Tensor Core的整数GEMM。

Result: 在RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell上验证，FP4上的Ozaki方案II在性能上与现有FP8实现相当，在大问题规模（16384³）时实际超越了FP8实现。当FP4 Tensor Core吞吐量为FP8两倍时，理论上FP4方案略有性能优势，结合内核优化后获得了实际的加速效果。

Conclusion: 成功在FP4 Tensor Core上实现了高精度矩阵乘法的Ozaki方案，证明了利用低精度硬件进行高精度计算的可行性，为未来硬件架构的高精度计算应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper proposes a method and its implementation for emulating FP64 matrix multiplication (DGEMM) by constructing, on FP4 (E2M1; 2 exponent bits and 1 mantissa bit) Tensor Cores, Ozaki schemes I and II, which realize high-precision matrix multiplication on low-precision arithmetic units. Prior implementations were based on INT8 and FP8, and the use of the faster FP4 had not been realized. The key property is that every FP4 value becomes an integer when doubled, and that shifting this integer set by multiples of 13 covers all integers. Converting an arbitrary integer into base-13 FP4 limbs by this property keeps intermediate sums error-free in FP32 accumulators, which makes FP4 Tensor Cores usable for Ozaki schemes I and II. By the same principle, the integer GEMM of INT8 Tensor Cores can also be emulated bit-exactly on FP4 Tensor Cores. When FP4 Tensor Cores have twice the throughput of FP8, Ozaki scheme II on FP4 theoretically achieves slightly higher performance than its FP8 counterpart. This paper further proposes kernel implementation optimizations raising the attained fraction of peak performance, obtaining a measured speedup on top of the theoretical advantage. We verify this on an RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell, achieving performance competitive with that of an existing FP8-based implementation of Ozaki scheme II, and actually exceeding it at a large problem size ($16384^3$).

</details>


### [5] [StateFlow: Sequence Pipeline Parallelism for Long-Context Modeling with Linear Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06838)
*Wenxuan Zhao,Yingfa Chen,Xu Han,Wenjing Han,Tianbo Huang,Zhiyu Li,Ao Sun,Jingheng Xu,Lin Gan,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了StateFlow系统，通过序列流水线并行和多种优化技术，显著提升了线性递归模型和混合模型在长序列训练中的吞吐量和内存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文训练对大语言模型日益重要，线性注意力和状态空间模型虽提高了效率，但递归和混合模型的长序列训练并行化仍具挑战性。

Method: StateFlow将序列分块并通过边界状态和梯度传播来调度执行，以减少激活值存活时间；采用Profile引导的非均匀分块来平衡混合模型中的计算负载，并重叠状态转移与周围计算。

Result: 在高达32B参数、256K上下文长度的模型上，相比传统流水线并行，吞吐量提升达2.22倍，内存减少达2.45倍，使原本不可行的配置变得可行。

Conclusion: StateFlow有效解决了线性递归模型长序列训练的并行化难题，显著提升了训练效率，为大模型长上下文训练提供了新的系统解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context training is increasingly important for large language models, and linear attention and state space models have become popular for improving long-context efficiency. However, efficiently parallelizing long-sequence training for recurrent and hybrid models remains challenging.
  We present StateFlow, a sequence pipeline parallelism system for models with linear recurrence. StateFlow partitions each sequence into chunks and schedules their execution while propagating boundary states and gradients across chunks, thereby reducing activation lifetimes and improving training throughput. StateFlow further uses profile-guided nonuniform chunking to balance recurrence and softmax attention computation in hybrid models, and overlaps state transitions that expose limited parallelism with surrounding computation. Applying StateFlow to models with up to 32B parameters and 256K context length, we achieve up to \(2.22\times\) throughput improvements and \(2.45\times\) memory reduction compared to conventional pipeline parallelism, enabling otherwise infeasible configurations.

</details>


### [6] [Stream Learning: Partition-Fair Gossip Learning Without Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06946)
*Fabien Mathieu,Alexandre Pham,Maria Gradinariu Potop-Butucaru,S{é}bastien Tixeuil*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Stream Learning协议族，其中最简单的Ri协议通过传输本地训练最少的分区给随机邻居，在正常情况下匹配PTGL性能，在对抗性故障场景下优于PTGL，且无需令牌计数器或元数据交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分区令牌流言学习（PTGL）协议虽然是最先进的方法，但其需要令牌计数器和邻居元数据交换来实现公平性。作者通过类比P2P直播流媒体系统，发现模型分区可以像视频块一样处理，分区年龄类似于块的稀缺性，这为重新设计分区调度提供了新视角。

Method: 将P2P直播流媒体中的块选择策略映射到流言学习，提出两阶段选择策略的设计空间（先选分区或先选邻居），从中实例化出十个具体协议组成的Stream Learning协议族。核心是Ri协议：传输本地训练最少的分区给均匀随机选择的邻居，使用分区年龄作为单一本地规则实现公平性。

Result: 在无故障工作负载下，Ri协议匹配PTGL性能。在对抗性30%永久崩溃（最佳性能节点）场景下，Ri在所有测试的完全图配置中匹配或优于PTGL，在最异构设置下（Dirichlet β=0.1），HAR数据集上差距达5.53%，MNIST上达5.41%。实验表明，基于分区年龄的单一本地规则实现的分区公平性足以解释性能差距，令牌速率控制和效用最大化在异构性条件下表现更差。

Conclusion: 简单的分区年龄规则足以实现有效的分区公平性，无需复杂的令牌机制或元数据交换。这一发现简化了流言学习协议设计，并在对抗性故障和异构数据分布场景下提供更好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In gossip learning, a network of nodes trains a shared model collaboratively, without a central coordinator, by repeatedly exchanging parts of their local models. The state-of-the-art protocol, Partitioned Token Gossip Learning (PTGL) of Heged{ü}s et al., splits the weight matrix into S fixed partitions and disseminates them using a token-based fairness mechanism coupled with per-neighbor metadata exchange. We revisit partition scheduling by analogy with peer-to-peer live streaming, where model partitions act as video chunks and partition age acts as chunk scarcity. The analogy yields a design space of two-stage selection strategies (partition first, or neighbor first), from which we instantiate ten concrete protocols collectively called Stream Learning. Our main finding is that the simplest of these protocols, which transmits the locally least-trained partition to a uniformly random neighbor (Ri), matches PTGL on fault-free workloads while requiring neither token counters nor metadata exchange. Under an adversarial 30% permanent crash of the best-performing nodes, Ri matches or outperforms PTGL across all complete-graph configurations tested, with the gap reaching 5.53% on HAR and 5.41% on MNIST in the most heterogeneous regime (Dirichlet $β$ = 0.1). In our experiments, partition fairness, captured by a single local rule on partition age, accounts for the gap; token-based rate control and utility maximization do not improve over this rule and, under heterogeneity, sit below it.

</details>


### [7] [A Self-Adaptive Extensible CEP framework for the Cloud-Edge Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06966)
*Olaf Markus Link,Sukanya Bhowmik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个具有负载调节功能的可扩展自适应复杂事件处理（CEP）框架，用于解决云边连续体中异构节点上的动态负载不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CEP系统在云边连续体的异构节点上部署操作符图时，面临动态变化的工作负载挑战。负载分布不均衡可能导致个别操作符因资源不足而无法适应增加的工作负载，从而导致处理过载。因此，需要一种能够实时检测并适应云边连续体各节点动态变化工作负载的框架。

Method: 提出了一种新的可扩展自适应CEP框架，该框架具有负载调节功能，能够帮助操作符在面对过载时继续工作。该框架能够实时检测和适应动态变化的工作负载，从而支持在异构和/或资源受限环境中部署操作符。

Result: 该框架能够在资源受限或异构环境中，即使面对过载情况，也能使操作符继续工作，实现了对动态变化工作负载的实时检测和适应能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应CEP框架通过负载调节功能有效解决了云边连续体中异构节点上的动态负载不平衡问题，为在资源受限环境中部署CEP操作符提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional complex event processing (CEP) systems focus on extracting information out of simple events in the input event streams to detect complex event patterns. In the context of CEP, a computing unit that executes a complex data transformation task is called an operator. Typically, a CEP system consists of an operator graph that could be distributed over the cloud-edge continuum. However, an operator graph distributed over heterogeneous nodes comes with its own set of challenges. In fact, the nodes of an operator graph are typically subjected to dynamically changing workloads. Unbalanced load distributions across heterogeneous nodes may lead to processing overloads in individual operators that do not have enough resources to properly adapt to an increasing workload. As a result, a framework that can detect and adapt to dynamically changing workload in real time across the various nodes of the cloud-edge continuum becomes paramount. To this end, the goal of this work is to propose a new extensible self-adaptive CEP framework with load regulatory features that help operators continue working even in face of an overload, thus facilitating the deployment of operators in a heterogeneous and/or resource-limited environment.

</details>


### [8] [A Kubernetes Scheduler Plugin for Cluster-Wide Placement Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06987)
*Henrik Daniel Christensen,Saverio Giallorenzo,Jacopo Mauro*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OPSche是一个开源的Kubernetes调度框架插件，允许外部求解器与默认调度器协同工作，实现集群范围内的全局放置决策，显著改善资源碎片化和集群使用率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes默认调度器采用快速、本地的放置决策，导致资源碎片化、集群使用率降低和过度配置。虽然外部求解器可以计算全局放置计划，但在上游集群中执行这些计划面临诸多挑战：Kubernetes缺乏原生跨节点抢占机制、不协调的并发调度导致不一致性、替换默认调度器会使部署脱离上游更新周期。

Method: 提出OPSche，一个开源的Kubernetes调度框架插件，通过协调的框架钩子原子性地验证和执行求解器生成的计划。支持三种触发模式：调度失败触发（工作负载无法放置时）、周期性触发（固定时间间隔）、稳定队列触发（待处理工作负载集稳定时）。每种模式都有阻塞变体，用于更精细地调整放置质量、延迟和干扰。OPSche与约束优化求解器配对使用。

Result: 在广泛的集群配置集上验证了可行性，资源使用率提升最高达3.0%，调度延迟改善超过一秒。

Conclusion: OPSche成功实现了外部求解器与Kubernetes默认调度器的协同工作，在不切断上游更新周期的前提下解决了资源碎片化问题，为容器编排系统提供了更优的集群资源管理方案。

Abstract: The default scheduler of Kubernetes, the state-of-the-art container orchestrator, uses fast, local placement decisions. Unfortunately, this design leads to resource fragmentation, reduced cluster usage, and overprovisioning. External solvers can compute global placement plans, but enforcing these plans in upstream clusters is hard. Kubernetes provides no native cross-node preemption, uncoordinated concurrent scheduling leads to inconsistencies, and replacing the default scheduler would sever deployments from upstream cycles. We present OPSche, an open-source Kubernetes Scheduling Framework plugin where external solvers can drive cluster-wide placement decisions in concert with the default scheduler. OPSche atomically validates and enforces solver-produced plans through coordinated framework hooks and supports three trigger modes: scheduling-failure, periodic, and stable-queue -- resp. triggered when a workload cannot be placed, at fixed time intervals, when the set of pending workloads stabilises. Each mode has a blocking variant for a finer tuning of placement quality, latency, and disruption. We pair OPSche with a constraint-based optimisation solver, showing its feasibility across a broad set of cluster configurations and reporting improvements of resource usage by up to 3.0% and scheduling latency by more than a second.

</details>


### [9] [HiSparse: Scaling Sparse-Attention Decoding with Hierarchical KV Cache Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07009)
*Zhiqiang Xie,Zhangheng Huang,Tingwei Huang,Ziyi Xu,Ruiyang Ma,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出HiSparse，一种针对稀疏注意力解码的分层KV缓存系统，通过将完整KV历史保留在主机内存并使用固定大小的GPU缓存，解决了长上下文LLM服务中的内存瓶颈问题，在保持模型输出不变的前提下显著提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Top-k稀疏注意力机制虽然减少了每步计算量，但服务系统仍需将完整KV缓存保留在GPU HBM中以保证所有位置可被选择。这导致请求的内存开销仍随上下文长度线性增长，解码在计算资源耗尽前就遇到容量瓶颈，且KV缓存超出HBM的上下文无法被服务。

Method: HiSparse采用分层KV缓存设计：将每个请求的完整KV历史保存在主机内存，使用固定大小的GPU缓存限制解码内存占用；通过融合CUDA内核在解码CUDA图中完成命中检测、LRU替换和主机到设备的数据获取；对于跨层共享选择权重的模型，实施精确的逐层预取以隐藏约一半的缺失开销。该方法仅改变KV放置位置，不改变模型输出。

Result: 在H200、B200和GH200平台上，针对DSA、NSA和Quest三种稀疏注意力架构进行评估。HiSparse在长上下文工作负载上将峰值生成吞吐量提升高达4.7倍，同时保持相当的单token延迟，在高负载下减少首个token的生成时间。无IO预言机实验表明解析机制本身不增加可测量的单token开销，主机-设备IO是有限驻留的唯一代价。

Conclusion: HiSparse成功解决了稀疏注意力服务中的内存容量瓶颈问题，实现了GPU显存占用与上下文长度的解耦，使长上下文LLM服务能够在有限GPU内存下高效运行，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Top-k sparse attention makes long-context LLM decoding cheap to compute: each step reads only a few thousand selected KV entries rather than the full context. Serving systems, however, typically keep the entire KV cache in GPU HBM so that every position stays selectable, so a request's memory bill still grows with its full context length--decoding hits a capacity wall long before it runs out of compute, and a context whose KV cache exceeds HBM cannot be served at all. We present HiSparse, an exact, indexer-agnostic hierarchical KV cache for sparse-attention serving. HiSparse keeps each request's full KV history in host memory and bounds its decode footprint with a small, fixed-size GPU cache; a fused CUDA kernel resolves each layer's selections--hit detection, LRU replacement, and host-to-device fetches--inside the decode CUDA graph; and, for models that share selections across layers, exact layer-wise prefetching hides roughly half of the remaining miss overhead. Because only KV placement changes, model outputs are unchanged. HiSparse is merged into upstream SGLang and evaluated across three sparse-attention families (DSA, NSA, and Quest) on H200, B200, and GH200 platforms: it improves peak generation throughput by up to 4.7x on long-context workloads while preserving comparable per-token latency and reducing time-to-first-token at high load--and a no-IO oracle shows the resolution mechanism itself adds no measurable per-token cost, leaving host-device IO as the only price of bounded residency.

</details>


### [10] [Scalable High-Fidelity Macromolecular Docking for GPU-Accelerated Supercomputers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07078)
*Xiangyu Meng,Peng Chen,Mingzhen Li,Jianmin Wang,Sen Wang,Guangming Tan,Weile Jia,Mohamed Wahib,Tao Luo,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了SparkleDock，一个基于GPU的GSO柔性分子对接框架，实现了比LightDock高达18.9倍的单卡加速和百倍规模扩展，将对接时间从数小时缩短至秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有柔性分子对接工具（如LightDock）虽然精度高，但计算成本过高，难以规模化应用；GSO算法存在并行度有限、计算不规则和负载不均问题，无法在GPU超算上高效执行。

Method: 重新设计GSO算法以在萤火虫代理层面暴露细粒度并行性；将主导的能量评分计算重构为Tensor Core兼容的矩阵运算形式；引入基于性能模型的调度策略实现负载均衡和跨GPU的外存扩展。

Result: 在单张A100和H100 GPU上分别实现9.7倍和18.9倍加速；在512 GPU规模下实现超百倍加速，将对接时间从小时级降至秒级。

Conclusion: SparkleDock实现了大规模、高保真虚拟筛选的近实时柔性对接能力，突破了柔性对接在规模化应用中的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Flexible macromolecular docking offers high-fidelity predictions of biomolecular interactions, but remains prohibitively expensive at scale. Among existing approaches, LightDock leverages Glowworm Swarm Optimization (GSO) for accuracy, yet suffers from limited parallelism, irregular computation, and severe load imbalance, preventing efficient execution on GPU supercomputers. We present SparkleDock, a scalable GSO-based docking framework enabling near-real-time flexible docking. We redesign GSO to expose massive fine-grained parallelism at the glowworm-agent level, and restructure the dominant energy scoring computation into a Tensor Core-compatible formulation, enabling efficient execution of irregular pairwise interactions through structured matrix operations. We further introduce a performance-model-driven scheduling for load balancing and out-of-core scaling across GPUs. SparkleDock achieves 9.7 $\times$ and 18.9 $\times$ speedups over LightDock on single A100 and H100 GPU, and delivers over two orders of magnitude acceleration at scale. On 512 GPUs, it reduces docking time from hours to seconds, enabling large-scale, high-fidelity virtual screening previously impractical with flexible docking.

</details>


### [11] [Fast end-to-end cloud application cold-start with initscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07358)
*Ariel Szekely,Robert Morris,M. Frans Kaashoek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出initscripts机制，通过重叠云平台的setup步骤与应用的initialization步骤，实现无代码修改的情况下将无服务器应用冷启动加速1.67-1.81倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器函数通常运行时间短且冷启动频繁，启动延迟往往主导执行延迟。启动延迟分为平台setup（下载二进制、创建隔离环境）和应用initialization（连接服务、协调工作、下载输入），两者串行执行导致延迟较长。

Method: 提出initscripts机制，提供可脚本化接口让开发者向云平台指定应用初始化例程。平台可并行执行initscript与应用setup，通过重叠setup和initialization步骤减少启动延迟；应用启动后，initscript将初始化结果传递给应用完成引导。

Result: 在ServerlessBench Python应用上实现平均1.67倍冷启动加速（无需修改应用）；无服务器图像识别工作负载加速1.81倍；对etcd和memcached等现成微服务同样有效。

Conclusion: Initscripts提供了一种有效的无服务器冷启动优化方案，通过并行化平台setup与应用initialization实现显著加速，且无需修改现有应用代码，具有良好通用性。

Abstract: Serverless functions are a popular way of deploying cloud applications. Because many of these functions are short- running and experience frequent cold-starts, start latencies often dominate their execution latency. Start latency can be broken down into two components: setup and initialization. Setup involves steps the cloud platform takes when starting an application, such as downloading its binary and creating an isolated execution environment. Initialization involves steps the application takes after it has started running but before it can do useful work, such as connecting to other services, coordinating to claim work, and downloading inputs.
  This paper contributes initscripts, which provide a script- able interface for developers to specify their application's initialization routine to the cloud platform. The platform can then run an application's initscript and reduce application start latency by overlapping setup and initialization steps. Once the application is up and running, the initscript bootstraps the application by transferring initialization results to it. Using initscripts, we were able to speed up cold-starts of several of the ServerlessBench Python applications by an average 1.67X with no modifications to the application. Initscripts also speed up start times for a serverless image recognition workload by 1.81X, and off-the-shelf microservices like etcd and memcached.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

### [12] [A Low-Latency ASIC Architecture for Real-Time Line Segment Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06439)
*Amir Hossein Jalilvand,Parsa Hassani Shariat Panahi,M. Hassan Najafi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于步长算法的低延迟ASIC架构用于实时线段检测，在45nm工艺下实现VGA分辨率325 FPS，功耗25.54 mW，面积0.412 mm²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法精度高但资源消耗大难以部署于资源受限平台，经典算法效率高但存在内容相关的延迟问题，需要一种适用于边缘计算的高效实时线段检测硬件方案。

Method: 基于步长算法设计全流水线ASIC架构，引入五个专用优化：基于寄存器的行缓冲与数据复用、基于MCM的无乘法器滤波、8类角度量化、类CAM关联存储器实现单周期匹配、优化的重复去除机制。

Result: 在45nm CMOS工艺下综合，VGA分辨率达325 FPS，Full HD达48 FPS，功耗25.54 mW，面积0.412 mm²；125 MHz时VGA吞吐提升至406 FPS，功耗31.48 mW。相比90nm Line Hough Transform ASIC实现，功耗降低49%，帧率提升超过1.6倍。

Conclusion: 该架构适用于需要实时性能、低功耗和小面积的边缘计算应用，成功实现了每时钟周期处理一个像素的确定性延迟。

Abstract: Line segment detection is a critical preprocessing step in embedded vision applications such as autonomous navigation, visual SLAM, and industrial inspection. Deep learning methods achieve high accuracy but require substantial resources, limiting their deployment on resource-constrained platforms. Classical algorithms are efficient but exhibit content-dependent latency. This paper presents a low-latency ASIC architecture for real-time line segment detection. The proposed design is based on the step-length algorithm and incorporates five ASIC-specific features: register-based line buffering with data reuse, multiplierless MCM-based filtering, 8-class angle quantization, a CAM-like associative memory for single-cycle matching, and an optimized duplicate removal mechanism. The architecture is fully pipelined and processes one pixel per clock cycle with deterministic latency. Synthesized in a 45nm CMOS process, the design achieves 325 FPS at VGA resolution and 48 FPS at Full HD, with 25.54 mW power consumption and 0.412 mm\textsuperscript{2} area. At 125 MHz, the throughput increases to 406 FPS at VGA resolution with 31.48 mW power consumption. Compared with a 90nm ASIC implementation based on the Line Hough Transform, the proposed design reduces power consumption by 49\% and delivers over 1.6 times higher frame rate. The architecture is well suited for edge-computing applications requiring real-time performance, low power, and minimal area.

</details>


### [13] [Retention-Aware RISC-V ISA Extension and Memory Controller on FPGA for MLC NVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06725)
*Mina Ibrahim,Martel Shokry,Lokesh Siddhu,Lars Bauer,Hassan Nassar,Joerg Henkel*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多级单元非易失性存储器（MLC NVM）的新型控制器架构和RISC-V指令集扩展，通过在写入速度和保持时间之间进行权衡优化，在FPGA上实现了硬件原型，显著降低了硬件开销并提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLC NVM技术能够在写入延迟和保持时间之间提供权衡（快速写入导致较低保持时间，慢速写入获得较高保持时间），但现有研究缺乏在硬件层面验证和原型实现这种权衡机制的系统级方案。

Method: 1）设计基于有限状态机和AXI内存映射接口的自定义NVM控制器，支持增强突发传输以最小化延迟；2）在RISC-V指令集中引入fast-store指令以提升写入性能；3）设计专用AXI从设备外设，支持位重要性感知写入——关键位（如MSB）采用慢速高保持写入，非关键位（如LSB）采用快速低保持写入。

Result: 控制器相比传统设计减少30%硬件开销；fast-store指令对流式工作负载性能提升超过7%，硬件开销小于0.08%；位级AXI外设LUT利用率在64x64矩阵下低于3.5%，32x32尺寸下低于1%，适合集成到大型SoC中。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构成功实现了MLC NVM写入速度与保持时间的系统级优化，在不损害数据可靠性的前提下显著提升了性能并降低了硬件开销，验证了该方法在SoC集成中的可行性。

Abstract: Non-volatile memory (NVM) technologies, particularly Multi-Level Cell (MLC) NVMs, offer significant potential for increasing memory density. MLC NVMs provide a tradeoff between write latency and retention time, where faster writes/stores result in lower retention and slower writes yield higher retention. However, limited work has been done to validate and prototype NVM-based systems in hardware, leveraging this tradeoff at the system level. In this paper, we present a novel memory controller architecture and a RISC-V instruction set extension to optimize MLC NVM write operations by balancing speed and retention time. Our custom NVM controller, built around a finite state machine with an AXI memory-mapped interface, efficiently manages read/write operations with enhanced burst transfers, minimizing latency. Additionally, we introduce a fast-store instruction in RISC-V to increasing write performance while addressing retention limitations. Further, we design a dedicated AXI slave peripheral that supports bit-significance-aware writes: critical bits (e.g., MSBs) are written using slower, high-retention writes, while non-critical bits (e.g., LSBs) use faster, low-retention writes to help enhance performance without compromising data reliability. These enhancements are implemented in hardware on an FPGA platform. Experimental results show that our controller reduces hardware overhead by 30% compared to conventional designs, and the fast-store instruction improves performance by over 7% for streaming workloads with less than 0.08% hardware overhead. The bit-wise AXI peripheral has a LUT utilization staying below 3.5% even for 64x64 matrices, and under 1% for 32x32 sizes, making it viable for integration into larger SoCs.

</details>


### [14] [HLSmith: An Expert-Guided Agentic Framework for C/C++-to-HLS Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06791)
*Yuebo Luo,Ahmad Sedigh Baroughi,Philip Stachura,Le Chen,Venkatram Vishwanath,Zhenman Fang,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了HLSmith框架，通过结合HLS优化专家知识库、分阶段反馈驱动的编排流程和模型适应管道，将C/C++程序转换为优化的HLS加速器，在PolyBench上相比ChatHLS实现4.24倍几何平均加速，设计正确率达100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特定应用的FPGA加速器开发成本高昂，即使使用高层次综合(HLS)仍需大量硬件专业知识。现有大语言模型虽然具备强大的软件生成能力，但缺乏将C/C++程序转换为高性能HLS设计所需的硬件直觉和过程知识，难以识别有效架构、遵循专家优化流程并一致地应用硬件变换。

Method: HLSmith框架包含三个核心组件：(1)HLS优化专家知识库，编码了受保护的变换规则、适用性和前提条件以及需要避免的危险情况；(2)基于专家HLS开发实践的分阶段反馈驱动编排流程，指导智能体完成综合、瓶颈分析和优化；(3)工具引导的模型适应管道，将商业前沿模型的优化轨迹转换为微调开放权重LLM的训练数据。

Result: 在PolyBench基准测试上相比ChatHLS实现4.24倍几何平均加速，设计正确率达100%（软件和RTL仿真均通过），而ChatHLS仅有57%的有效设计率。商业前沿模型和开放权重模型分别达到高达252倍和138倍的加速。

Conclusion: HLSmith成功地将专家知识与大语言模型结合，有效解决了C/C++程序到高性能HLS加速器的转换问题，显著提升了设计效率和性能，证明了专家引导方法在硬件加速器设计自动化中的有效性。

Abstract: Application-specific FPGA accelerators offer substantial performance and energy-efficiency gains across many application domains, but developing them is costly, often requiring months of specialized effort. Even with high-level synthesis (HLS), designers still need extensive hardware expertise to build high-performance accelerators. Although large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong software-generation capabilities, even frontier models lack the hardware intuition and procedural knowledge needed to reliably translate baseline C/C++ programs into high-performance HLS designs: they struggle to identify effective architectures, follow the optimization processes used by HLS experts, and apply hardware transformations consistently across diverse kernels. We present HLSmith, an expert-guided framework for translating C/C++ programs into optimized HLS accelerators. HLSmith combines three components: an HLS optimization expertise library that encodes guarded transformation recipes, their applicability and prerequisite conditions, and unsafe cases to avoid; a staged, feedback-driven orchestration flow modeled on expert HLS development practice that guides agents through synthesis, bottleneck analysis, and optimization; and a tool-grounded model-adaptation pipeline that converts optimization trajectories from commercial frontier models into training data for fine-tuning open-weight LLMs. We evaluate HLSmith on PolyBench against ChatHLS, a leading prior agent-orchestration framework for HLS accelerator development. HLSmith achieves a geometric mean speedup of 4.24x over ChatHLS while producing functionally correct designs, in both software and RTL simulation, for every benchmark, compared with ChatHLS's 57% valid-design rate. It further reaches speedups of up to 252x and 138x with commercial frontier models and open-weight models, respectively.

</details>


### [15] [G-Power: Architecture-level GPU Power Modeling with Aggregated Knowledge Foundations from Known GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06870)
*Qijun Zhang,Yao Lu,Shang Liu,Mengming Li,Chen Zhang,Dongbo Wang,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出G-Power框架，利用已知GPU芯片的额外知识来解决微基准测试训练数据局限性问题，在四个现代NVIDIA GPU上实现平均14% MAPE和0.88相关系数，显著优于AccelWattch。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有架构级GPU功耗模型（如AccelWattch）仅使用简单微基准测试作为训练数据，由于功耗标签收集耗时，导致模型精度较低。随着芯片复杂度增加，功耗效率成为现代GPU重要设计目标，需要快速功耗评估，迫切需要高精度架构级GPU功耗模型。

Method: 提出G-Power架构级GPU功耗建模框架，采用三阶段算法：1）使用额外已知芯片进行预训练；2）基于注意力机制的聚合，捕获相似性以利用已知芯片的知识基础；3）在目标GPU上进行微调。该方法利用已知GPU芯片提供额外知识，构建聚合知识基础。

Result: 在四个现代NVIDIA GPU上评估，G-Power实现平均14% MAPE和0.88相关系数R，相比AccelWattch，MAPE降低22%，R提高0.36，展现出高精度和强相关性。

Conclusion: G-Power通过利用已知GPU芯片的额外知识有效解决了微基准测试训练数据的局限性问题，为现代GPU功耗优化提供了高精度架构级功耗建模方案，显著提升了功耗预测准确性。

Abstract: Graphics Processing Units (GPUs) have been serving as critical computation resources for large-scale parallel computations. With increasing chip complexity, power efficiency has become an important design objective for modern GPUs. GPU power optimization relies on fast power evaluation, requiring architecture-level GPU power model. However, because of the time-consuming power label collection, only simple microbenchmarks are adopted for training. The limitation of microbenchmarks as training data incurs low accuracy for existing architecture-level GPU power models like AccelWattch.
  To address the limitation of microbenchmarks as training data, we propose G-Power, an architecture-level GPU power modeling framework that utilizes additional known GPU chips to provide additional knowledge. G-Power utilizes the aggregated knowledge foundation from additional known GPU chips and then performs fine-tuning on our target GPU. To provide foundations with additional known GPU chips and capture the similarity to utilize these foundations for fine-tuning, G-Power adopts a three-phase algorithm consisting of 1) pre-training with additional known chips, 2) attention-inspired aggregation, and 3) fine-tuning on our target GPU. We evaluate G-Power on four modern NVIDIA GPUs, demonstrating high accuracy. G-Power can achieve a low MAPE of 14% and a high correlation coefficient R of 0.88 on average, which are 22% lower MAPE and 0.36 higher R than AccelWattch.

</details>


### [16] [Rethinking Unified Memory for NPU-PIM Systems: Dual-View Memory for Dynamic Inference of LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06989)
*Shixin Zhao,Lian Liu,Tianhua Han,Mengdi Wang,Yinhe Han,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出PFM，一种面向NPU-PIM异构系统的双视图内存管理方案，通过解耦物理布局与逻辑视图并引入动态调度机制，解决了LLM推理中因执行设备动态变化导致的内存带宽低效问题，实现了最高2.32倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NPU-PIM异构架构的统一内存设计假设张量绑定于固定执行设备，采用静态、设备偏向的数据映射。然而，现代LLM负载因阶段变化（如prefill与decode）和动态行为（如MoE路由），同一张量的最优执行设备会在运行时动态改变。这种动态执行导致静态映射与访问模式失配，造成显著的带宽利用率不足和性能损失。

Method: 提出PFM（PIM-as-Flexible-Memory）双视图内存系统：1）解耦物理数据布局与访问者可见的逻辑视图，采用联合优化的物理布局存储数据，并向NPU和PIM暴露不同的逻辑解释，实现跨设备高效访问且无需数据复制或重布局；2）设计访问者感知的地址翻译机制；3）引入运行时调度机制，以支持LLM负载波动及最优执行设备动态变化场景下的动态执行。

Result: 在多种LLM模型上的评估表明，PFM将端到端吞吐量提升高达2.32倍，有效解决了动态执行场景下的内存带宽低效问题，验证了其作为NPU-PIM系统统一内存管理方案的有效性与广泛适用性。

Conclusion: PFM通过物理布局与逻辑视图的解耦设计，以及配套的地址翻译和运行时调度机制，成功应对了LLM推理中执行设备动态变化带来的内存管理挑战，为NPU-PIM异构系统提供了一种高效、通用的统一内存管理解决方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous architectures that combine neural processing unit (NPU) and processing-in-memory (PIM) are increasingly adopted to accelerate LLM inference. Prior work focuses on building a unified memory that allows NPUs and PIM to share data without duplication. However, these designs implicitly assume that each tensor is bound to a fixed execution device, and therefore rely on static, device-biased data mappings.
  We observe that this assumption does not hold in modern LLM workloads. Due to phase changes (e.g., prefill vs. decode) and dynamic behaviors such as MoE routing, the optimal execution device for the same tensor can change at runtime. Under such dynamic execution, device-biased mappings become mismatched to access patterns, leading to substantial bandwidth underutilization and performance loss. This paper presents PFM (PIM-as-Flexible-Memory), a dual-view memory system that decouples physical data layout from accessor-visible logical views. PFM stores data in a jointly optimized physical layout and exposes different logical interpretations to NPUs and PIM, enabling efficient access across devices without data duplication or relayout. We further design accessor-aware address translation and runtime scheduling mechanisms to support dynamic execution when LLM workloads fluctuate and the optimal execution device dynamically changes. Our evaluation across LLMs shows that PFM improves end-to-end throughput by up to 2.32$\times$, demonstrating its effectiveness and broad applicability as a unified memory management solution for NPU-PIM systems.

</details>


### [17] [Dual-Node NVIDIA DGX Spark over Tailscale: A Remote-Access Testbed for Distributed LLM Training and Cyber-Threat-Intelligence Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07226)
*Vasanth Iyer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文展示了在两台NVIDIA DGX Spark桌面级加速器上部署分布式NanoChat预训练的可行性验证，四天处理约6.53亿tokens，并进行了网络安全领域微调实验，证明了小型实验室基础设施可同时支持研究与教学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型AI系统使本地语言模型实验日益普及，但在桌面级加速器上进行多节点训练的实践证据仍然有限。作者希望填补这一空白，为小型实验室提供可复现的部署参考。

Method: 在两台各配备GB10 Grace Blackwell片上系统和128GB统一内存的NVIDIA DGX Spark系统上部署分布式NanoChat预训练。采用PyTorch torchrun、DDP和NCCL进行分布式配置，使用200 Gb/s QSFP56直连光纤链路和Tailscale网状VPN远程管理。模型为深度20的NanoChat，每节点本地批次大小32，2048 token上下文。同时构建网络安全微调数据集（77份CISA公告），使用Ollama托管LLM裁判进行17问题评估。

Result: 训练运行维持约69.4秒/步（约1890 tokens/s），四天处理约6.53亿tokens。网络安全特定类别评估分数提升，通用知识类别有所下降，总体从2.06升至2.29（0-10分制）。集群成功支持400级AI课程和CompTIA Security+ POGIL活动的查询引擎。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在桌面级加速器上进行分布式语言模型预训练的可行性，而非做出扩展效率声明。小型本地基础设施可同时服务研究和教学需求。作者提供了运行手册和脚本以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Compact AI systems make local language-model experimentation increasingly accessible, yet practical evidence for multi-node training on desktop-class accelerators remains limited. This report presents a proof-of-concept deployment of distributed NanoChat pretraining across two NVIDIA DGX Spark systems, each with a GB10 Grace Blackwell system-on-chip and 128 GB of unified memory, administered remotely over a Tailscale mesh VPN and connected for training by a dedicated 200 Gb/s QSFP56 direct fiber link. PyTorch torchrun, DDP, and NCCL were configured with one process per node, a depth-20 NanoChat model, a local batch size of 32 per node, and a 2,048-token context, giving a global batch of 131,072 tokens per step. The run sustained a step time of about 69.4 s (about 1,890 tokens/s), processing about 653 million tokens over four days. We document link configuration, container setup, interface binding, a step-zero evaluation bug that triggered NCCL timeouts, checkpointing, and troubleshooting lessons, as a reproducibility reference for small labs.
  We also built a cybersecurity fine-tuning dataset from 77 CISA advisories (338 training, 37 validation conversations) and ran a 17-question held-out evaluation comparing a baseline SFT checkpoint against a CTI-augmented checkpoint with an Ollama-hosted LLM judge. CTI-specific categories improved while general-knowledge categories regressed, for a small overall change from 2.06 to 2.29 on a 0-10 scale. The same cluster supports a 400-level AI course (CS 426) and a query engine for CompTIA Security+ POGIL activities in CBS 255, showing modest local infrastructure can serve both research and teaching. The study establishes feasibility rather than a scaling-efficiency claim, since single-node throughput used for comparison was estimated, not measured under matched conditions. Runbook and scripts are available (see Code Availability).

</details>

### [18] [Self-Healing 6G Networks-in-Network for Resilient Wireless Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06423)
*Daniel Lindenschmitt,Anton Schösser,Afnan Aghai,Philipp Schulz,Gerhard Fettweis,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文展示了用于未来6G网络的自主愈网中网（NiN）架构，通过将频谱异常检测与动态频谱管理紧密集成，实现了从干扰检测到频率重配的实时自愈能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来6G网络需要在动态无线电环境中管理多个自主子网络共享频率资源，而故障设备或故意干扰会导致通信中断，迫切需要快速自主的网络适应与自愈能力。

Method: 提出一种自愈的网中网架构，通过频谱扫描器持续监测频率谱并检测异常，将异常信息传递给动态频谱管理（DSM）模块，DSM自动识别合适频率资源并重新配置受影响的子网络，形成检测与管理的闭环控制。

Result: 在实时演示中，参与者可发起受控干扰并观察从异常检测到自主频率重配和网络恢复的完整自适应过程，验证了系统的实时自愈能力。

Conclusion: 将频谱监测与资源管理集成于单一控制回路中，可有效提升未来网中网实现的韧性，并为自主、频谱感知的网络提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Future 6G networks must manage increasingly dynamic radio environments in which multiple autonomous sub-networks (SNs) share frequency resources and adapt to changing operating conditions. In such scenarios, interference from faulty devices or intentional jamming can disrupt ongoing communications, making rapid and autonomous network adaptation essential. This demonstration presents a self-healing networks-in-network (NiN) architecture that closely integrates the detection of spectrum anomalies with dynamic spectrum management. A spectrum scanner continuously monitors the frequency spectrum and forwards detected anomalies to the DSM, which automatically identifies suitable frequency resources and reconfigures the affected SN. During the live demonstration, participants can initiate controlled disruptions and observe the entire adaptation process in real time, from anomaly detection to autonomous frequency reallocation and network recovery. The demonstrator illustrates how integrating spectrum monitoring and resource management into a single control loop can improve the resilience of future NiN implementations and demonstrates a practical approach to autonomous, spectrum-aware networking.

</details>


### [19] [FedTransKD-IDS: Robust Federated Transfer Learning with Knowledge Distillation for Intrusion Detection in IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06447)
*Mohammad Hosssein Gholamrezazadeh,Ahmadreza MontazerolghaemAhmadreza Montazerolghaem*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedTransKD-IDS框架，通过结合几何均值鲁棒聚合、联邦迁移学习和知识蒸馏技术，解决了物联网和5G网络环境中入侵检测系统在统计异质性和资源约束下的隐私保护与可扩展性问题，实现了99.18%的准确率和99.99%的召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式网络环境（特别是物联网基础设施和5G网络）中，入侵检测系统面临严格的隐私保护和可扩展性要求。虽然联邦学习通过避免数据集中化来保护隐私，但在边缘节点的统计异质性和资源约束条件下，其效率和稳定性显著下降。

Method: 提出FedTransKD-IDS框架，整合三种关键技术：1）基于几何均值的鲁棒聚合方法；2）联邦迁移学习；3）知识蒸馏。框架中协同训练的全局教师模型将其特征提取组件迁移至轻量级学生模型，实现结构化知识转移。

Result: 在异构数据集上的实验评估表明，该框架达到峰值检测性能，准确率为99.18%，召回率为99.99%，验证了结构化知识转移在联邦环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: FedTransKD-IDS框架成功解决了联邦学习环境下的统计异质性和资源约束问题，在保证隐私的前提下显著提升了入侵检测系统的稳定性和效率，为分布式网络环境中的安全防护提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In modern distributed network environments, particularly in Internet of Things infrastructures and 5G networks, stringent privacy preservation and scalability requirements have created significant challenges for intrusion detection systems. Although federated learning preserves privacy by preventing data centralization, its efficiency and stability is considerably degraded under severe statistical heterogeneity and resource constraints of edge nodes. To address these limitations, this study introduces the FedTransKD-IDS framework, which enhances both system stability and efficiency by integrating robust aggregation based on the geometric mean, federated transfer learning, and knowledge distillation. Within this framework, the collaboratively trained global teacher model transfers its feature extraction component to lightweight student models. Experimental evaluation on heterogeneous datasets demonstrates a peak detection performance, achieving an accuracy of 99. 18% and a recall of 99. 99%, thereby indicating the effectiveness of structured knowledge transfer in federated environments.

</details>


### [20] [Detecting and Characterizing Massively Shared IP Addresses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06517)
*Amanda Hsu,Paul Pearce,Frank Li,Arthur Berger,Philipp Richter*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究提出“大规模共享IP”概念，发现其承载了超过40%的IPv4流量，且集中于不到2%的活跃IP地址，呈增长趋势，对IP归属、黑名单、限速等机制有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IP地址因NAT、代理等原因被多设备/用户共享，大规模共享场景（如运营商级NAT）会对基于IP的归属判定、黑名单、限速等机制产生重大影响，误判将波及大量用户和组织，因此需要全面刻画和检测这种现象。

Method: 利用大型CDN的流量数据，基于流量形态的日变化模式，在全球范围内检测并刻画“大规模共享IP”地址，并结合蜂窝连接、双栈能力、云托管代理等网络特征进行上下文分析。

Result: 发现大规模共享IP承载了IPv4总流量的40%以上，集中在不到2%的活跃地址；地理分布呈明显差异，小国比例尤高；IPv6中此类地址较少但在移动运营商中有例外；部分云网络代理服务存在大规模共享IP；该类流量比例随时间持续上升。

Conclusion: 大规模IP共享是IPv4流量的主要组成部分并呈增长态势，现有IP依赖型机制需正视其广泛存在与潜在影响，研究为理解全球IP共享现状提供了广泛视角。

Abstract: IP addresses are commonly shared across devices and users for a variety of reasons, including NAT and proxies. These technologies operate at different scales, from residential NATs that share an IP address across devices in a home to large-scale Carrier Grade NATs that share hundreds or thousands of users on a single IP. Cases of large-scale IP sharing are distinct as they have significant implications for IP-based mechanisms such as attribution, blocklisting, and rate-limiting, where the consequences of mishandling affect a large quantity of end-users and organizations.
  In this work, we detect and characterize IP addresses shared at large scales, which we coin massively shared. Leveraging diurnal patterns in traffic shape, we use data from a large CDN to characterize these IPs globally. We broadly find that massive IP sharing is responsible for a large fraction of IPv4 traffic, concentrated in a small fraction of address space, with over 40% of total traffic coming from less than 2% of active IP addresses. We observe distinct patterns in deployment geographically, with particularly high rates of massively shared traffic from some smaller countries. Comparatively, in IPv6, we find far fewer massively shared addresses with some surprising exceptions among mobile providers. We additionally contextualize these addresses by other network characteristics, including identifying cellular connectivity and dual-stack capabilities, and identifying several instances of massively shared IPs in proxy services hosted on cloud networks. Finally, we find that rates of massively shared traffic are increasing over time, predicting future reliance on these technologies. Our work contextualizes the state of IP sharing, providing a uniquely broad perspective globally.

</details>


### [21] [Improving the Energy Efficiency of High Throughput Computing: A Measurement-Based Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06622)
*Damu Ding,Xinpeng Hong,Alastair Dewhurst,James Walder,Daniel Schien,David Greenwood,Noa Zilberman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过实测案例研究数据中心高通量计算场景下的服务器能耗问题，分析了降低能耗的障碍和服务器配置调整的影响，提出了兼顾性能和碳排放的节能建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心的巨大能耗已成为成本和碳排放的关注焦点，而服务器在不同运行条件下的功耗理解是提高能效的关键，因此需要深入研究实际运营环境中的服务器能耗特性及优化策略。

Method: 采用基于实测的案例研究方法，分析运营数据中心的功耗信息，并针对代表性高通量工作负载进行针对性的节能技术测量实验。

Result: 识别出数据中心运营者在最小化能耗和碳排放过程中面临的障碍，揭示了服务器配置调整对作业处理能耗的影响机制，获得了功耗降低技术的实测数据。

Conclusion: 提出了可操作的降低服务器能耗的建议方案，这些建议在降低能耗的同时兼顾了性能表现和碳排放影响，为数据中心运营者提供了实用的节能优化指导。

Abstract: The significant energy consumed by data centers has become a concern both for costs and associated carbon emissions. In particular, the energy efficiency of servers is a key consideration for data center operators, and understanding servers' power consumption under different operating conditions is an important aspect of it. In this paper, we present a measurement-based case study of high-throughput computing. We analyze power usage information of an operational data center, combined with focused measurements of power reduction techniques for a representative high-throughput workload. The study points out the obstacles encountered by data center operators in their efforts to minimize energy consumption and carbon emissions, and discusses the impact of server configuration adjustments on the energy consumption of processing jobs. We offer actionable recommendations for decreasing the energy usage of servers, while considering both performance and carbon emissions.

</details>


### [22] [A Parameter-Specific Retrieval and Knowledge-Guided Reasoning Framework for LLM-Based GPSR Optimization in FANETs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06760)
*Zhipeng Lin,Bin Duo,Tong Liu,Jie Lin,Jianting Yuan,Xiaojun Yuan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PMKR-GPSR框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）实现GPSR协议参数的自适应优化，通过参数特定的多索引检索机制和知识引导的约束图，提高了高动态FANET环境下的网络性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于GPSR的FANET协议在高度动态环境下难以自适应调整路由参数（如hello间隔、多路径数量、贪婪转发权重）。大语言模型作为新兴的AI技术，在智能决策方面展现出潜力，为GPSR参数的自适应调整提供了新机会。然而，将LLM应用于GPSR仍面临无关经验检索和缺乏协议约束等挑战。

Method: 提出了PMKR-GPSR框架，包含两个核心创新：(1)设计参数特定的多索引检索机制，为LLM提供参数相关的经验，同时减少无关信息的干扰；(2)构建知识引导的约束图，确保路由参数满足依赖规则和优化约束。该框架实现了与协议一致的路由参数自适应调整。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在高移动性FANET环境下，PMKR-GPSR实现了更高的数据包投递率和更低的端到端延迟。

Conclusion: PMKR-GPSR成功地将大语言模型应用于GPSR协议的参数优化，通过参数特定的检索机制和知识引导的约束，有效解决了高度动态环境下路由参数自适应调整的难题，显著提升了网络性能。

Abstract: Existing Greedy Perimeter Stateless Routing (GPSR)-based protocols for Flying Ad-Hoc Networks (FANETs) struggle to adapt routing parameters, such as hello interval, multi-path number, and greedy forwarding weights, under highly dynamic environments. As an emerging artificial intelligence technology, large language models (LLMs) show potential for intelligent decision-making, providing new opportunities for adaptive adjustment of GPSR parameters to improve network performance. However, applying LLMs to GPSR remains challenging due to irrelevant experience retrieval and the absence of protocol constraints. To address these issues, we propose a Parameter-Specific Multi-Index Retrieval and Knowledge-Guided Reasoning framework for adaptive GPSR optimization (PMKR-GPSR), an LLM-based framework that enables protocol-consistent routing parameter adaptation. We design a parameter-specific multi-index retrieval mechanism to provide LLMs with parameter-relevant experiences while reducing interference from irrelevant information. We further construct a knowledge-guided constraint graph to enforce that the routing parameters satisfy dependency rules and optimization constraints. Simulation results demonstrate that PMKR-GPSR achieves higher packet delivery ratio and lower end-to-end delay under high-mobility FANETs.

</details>


### [23] [EvoRIC: Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuned LLM-empowered RAN Intelligent Control Toward Autonomous O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06789)
*Lingyan Bao,Jemin Lee,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EvoRIC框架，通过分层架构和强化学习微调机制，解决了传统ML算法泛化能力有限和通用LLM缺乏领域知识的问题，在IAB网络中验证了其有效性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI在RAN应用中存在关键挑战：传统机器学习算法在不同网络拓扑下泛化能力有限，而通用大语言模型面临高计算需求且缺乏领域特定知识，亟需一种能够兼顾泛化能力和领域适应性的解决方案。

Method: 提出EvoRIC框架，采用分层架构：非实时RIC负责全局模型更新，近实时RIC负责本地执行；引入强化学习微调机制，将LLM作为近端策略优化（PPO）智能体中的演员，利用无线环境交互元组迭代更新LLM参数，使其语义推理与网络性能目标对齐。

Result: 在集成接入与回传（IAB）网络中验证了EvoRIC框架的泛化能力和有效性，证明了该方法能够动态赋予LLM领域特定的决策能力。

Conclusion: EvoRIC框架为实现自主O-RAN提供了新的技术路径，但仍面临开放性挑战，需要进一步研究以推动框架的持续演进和实际部署。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in applying artificial intelligence (AI) techniques to radio access network (RAN), critical challenges remain: traditional machine learning (ML) algorithms suffer from limited generalization across varying network topologies, whereas general-purpose large language models (LLMs) face high computational demands and lack domain-specific knowledge. To address these gaps, this article introduces the evolving RAN intelligent controller (RIC) (EvoRIC) framework, a hierarchical architecture that enables continuous evolution by leveraging a non-real-time RIC (non-RT RIC) for global model updates and a near-real-time RIC (near-RT RIC) for local execution, dynamically empowering LLMs with domain-specific decision-making capabilities. Within this framework, we employ a reinforcement learning-based fine-tuning (RLFT) mechanism where an LLM operates as an actor within a proximal policy optimization (PPO) agent. By leveraging the interaction tuples collected from the wireless environment, the LLM's parameters are iteratively updated to align semantic reasoning with rigorous network performance objectives. We evaluate the generalization and efficacy of the proposed EvoRIC framework within integrated access and backhaul (IAB) networks, and finally, discuss the open challenges and future directions of the EvoRIC framework toward realizing autonomous O-RAN.

</details>


### [24] [LYRA: Label-Free Structural Synchronization and Resource Allocation for UAV Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07392)
*Feng He,Alireza Furutanpey,Paolo Bellavista,Yu Qiu,Jiangchuan Liu,Jiannong Cao,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个针对UAV边缘系统的联合模型更新调度与资源分配框架，通过在线语义分歧率(OSDR)代理指标实现无标签语义评估，并利用Lyapunov引导的离散强化学习算法解决混合动作空间维度灾难和长期能量约束问题，在语义恢复效率和更新触发精度上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV边缘系统在执行关键任务时部署分层视觉模型，需要在低级环境损坏下自适应更新模型以维持推理可靠性。然而现有研究忽视了三个关键问题：模型更新的最佳时机、依赖实时专家标签的不切实际性，以及UAV显著的带宽和能量约束。

Method: 提出在线语义分歧率(OSDR)作为无标签语义评估的代理指标，实现细粒度的敏感性感知结构同步(SASS)；设计Lyapunov引导的离散强化学习算法，通过动作空间降维和将约束转化为虚拟队列稳定性问题，解决混合动作空间的维度灾难和长期能量预算约束。

Result: 基于真实流量轨迹的实验表明，该框架在语义恢复效率和更新触发精度方面始终优于代表性基线方法；在动态环境损坏场景下，满足长期能量预算的同时，将平均风险积压降低高达33.3%。

Conclusion: 所提出的联合模型更新调度与资源分配框架能够有效解决UAV边缘智能系统中的无标签语义评估、更新时机选择和资源约束问题，实现了长期语义保真度和资源效率的最大化，为动态环境下的UAV边缘智能系统提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: While deploying hierarchical vision models to process mission-critical tasks, UAV edge systems must adaptively update the models to sustain inference reliability under low-level environmental corruption. However, existing work has overlooked the optimal timing for model updates, the impracticality of relying on real-time expert labels, and the significant bandwidth and energy constraints of UAVs. This paper proposes a joint model update scheduling and resource allocation framework, aiming to maximize long-term semantic fidelity and resource efficiency of UAV edge intelligence systems. To address the challenge of label-free semantic evaluation, we formulate the Online Semantic Disagreement Rate (OSDR) as a proxy for timely update triggering, thereby enabling fine-grained Sensitivity-Aware Structural Synchronization (SASS). Furthermore, to overcome the curse of dimensionality in hybrid action spaces and effectively bound long-term energy budgets, we propose a Lyapunov-guided discrete reinforcement learning algorithm that performs action space dimensionality reduction and transforms constraints into virtual queue stability problems. The reported experimental results, based on real traffic traces, demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms representative baselines in semantic recovery efficiency and update triggering precision, by satisfying long-term energy budget and by reducing average risk backlog by up to 33.3\% in the dynamic environmental corruption scenario.

</details>

### [25] [Towards Multi-Label Graph Foundation Models: from Single-Vector Representation Learning to Multi-Semantic Basis Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06394)
*Dongxiao He,Jiayu Zhang,Jitao Zhao,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MSB-GFM框架，首次解决了跨域多标签节点分类问题，通过多语义基表示学习解决语义纠缠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型(GFMs)基于单标签假设，将多标签节点压缩为单一表示，导致语义纠缠问题；且现有方法仅在域内场景有效，缺乏跨域泛化能力。

Method: 提出多语义基表示学习范式，将每个多标签节点建模为语义基的适应性组合；设计语义-结构双通道架构，结合域对抗训练实现跨域知识迁移。

Result: 在跨域多标签节点分类任务上进行了大量实验，验证了模型的有效性。

Conclusion: MSB-GFM成功实现了跨域多标签节点分类，通过多语义基表示有效解决了语义纠缠问题，为图基础模型在多标签场景的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification is an important yet challenging task in graph learning, where nodes exhibit multiple semantics simultaneously. Existing methods for multi-label node classification can effectively model multiple labels, while only considering in-domain scenarios where the model needs to be trained and tested within the same graph domain, resulting in limited cross-domain generalization. Recently, Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable graph representations across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing GFMs are built upon single-label assumption, where all nodes are arbitrarily regarded as containing only one class of semantic and embedded into a single representation. For multi-label nodes, such a representation essentially approximates multiple semantics with a single point in the representation space, inevitably leading to semantic entanglement and making simultaneous discrimination of multiple labels difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Semantic Basis Graph Foundation Model (MSB-GFM), a framework for cross-domain multi-label node classification. Specifically, we introduce a multi-semantic basis representation learning paradigm that models each multi-label node as an adaptive composition of semantic bases, thereby enabling flexible representational capacity for modeling multiple semantics. Furthermore, we develop a semantic-structure dual-channel architecture with domain adversarial training for effective cross-domain knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model.

</details>


### [26] [EntropyMoE: Entropy-Aware Sparse Expert Routing for Tokenizer-Free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06398)
*Bo Liu,Muxuab Yu,Yu Zhang,Pengfei Gao,Yongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EntropyMoE，一种针对动态字节patch的混合专家架构，通过patch熵作为路由坐标实现了更优的压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字节级patch架构对所有patch应用相同的密集前馈计算，无法根据patch语义和粒度的变化调整模型容量，导致计算资源分配不够灵活。

Method: 提出EntropyMoE架构，将全局patch Transformer中的密集前馈模块替换为Top-K专家层；以每个动态patch作为专家路由的基本单元，根据其字节覆盖度计算工作负载；路由器直接从patch熵选择专家，利用动态patch构造的粒度信号组织稀疏计算；patch熵和长度联合定义特征空间来调节专家专业化。

Result: EntropyMoE在相同参数量的密集基线和稀疏基线对比中取得了最低的held-out bits-per-byte，同时保持了相当的下游任务准确率。

Conclusion: patch熵可作为稀疏条件计算的有效路由坐标，成功将混合专家建模扩展到基于tokenizer表示之外的领域。

Abstract: Recent byte-level large language models (LLMs) have made tokenizer-free modeling increasingly competitive by grouping bytes into dynamically sized patches. However, existing byte-patch architectures still apply the same dense feed-forward computation to every patch. This uniform computation cannot adapt model capacity to variations in patch semantics and granularity. We address this limitation with EntropyMoE, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for dynamic byte patches. EntropyMoE replaces the dense feed-forward modules in the global patch Transformer with Top-K expert layers. Each dynamic patch serves as the basic unit of expert routing, and its byte coverage determines its contribution to workload accounting. The router selects experts directly from patch entropy, using the same granularity signal that underlies dynamic patch construction to organize sparse computation. Patch entropy and length jointly define the feature space for regulating expert specialization. Experiments show that EntropyMoE achieves the lowest held-out bits-per-byte among matched dense and sparse baselines while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy. These results establish patch entropy as an effective routing coordinate for sparse conditional computation and extend Mixture-of-Experts modeling beyond tokenizer-based representations.

</details>


### [27] [Beyond Routing Weights: Faithful Response-Level Interpretation of Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models via Contribution Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06400)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CoCo（Contribution-Contrast）方法，通过具有最大贡献对比的chosen-rejected响应对来解释MoE奖励模型中的专家角色，相比现有方法更连贯、忠实且专门化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏MoE奖励模型仅通过路由权重解释专家行为，但这只能揭示专家接收哪些提示，无法说明专家如何判断响应，导致对专家行为的解释不完整。

Method: 提出Contribution-Contrast（CoCo）响应级解释方法，通过选择具有最大贡献对比的chosen-rejected响应对来刻画专家角色，联合捕获路由行为和偏好行为。

Result: 在自动评估和人类评估中，CoCo比基于路由、基于分数和基于稀疏自编码器的替代方案产生更连贯、忠实和专门化的解释，同时保持竞争性的奖励建模准确性。

Conclusion: 首次对MoE奖励模型的解释方法进行系统研究，证明了CoCo方法的有效性，为理解MoE奖励模型的预测机制提供了更好的解释工具。

Abstract: Reward models are central to learning from human preferences, yet identifying what drives their predictions remains challenging. Recent sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward models seek to improve interpretability by routing prompts to specialized experts and characterizing experts through examples with high routing weights. However, routing weights only reveal which prompts an expert $\textit{receives}$, not how it $\textit{judges}$ responses, providing only a partial account of expert behavior. We therefore propose $\textbf{Co}$ntribution-$\textbf{Co}$ntrast ($\textbf{CoCo}$) response-level interpretation, which faithfully characterizes experts' roles using chosen-rejected response pairs with the largest contribution contrasts, jointly capturing routing and preference behavior. Across automatic and human evaluations, CoCo yields more coherent, faithful, and specialized interpretations than router-based, score-based, and sparse autoencoder-based alternatives while maintaining competitive reward modeling accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of interpretation methods for MoE reward models.

</details>


### [28] [Interpretable Unsupervised Community Detection with LLM-Symbolized Structured Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06402)
*Aoting Zeng,Kai Wang,Jianwei Wang,Yuxiang Sun,Yizhang He,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LUCID，一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的无监督、免训练、可解释的社区检测方法，通过四阶段流程在真实数据集上实现了超越现有无监督和半监督方法的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于目标优化的社区检测方法难以处理复杂图结构，而深度学习方法虽然性能有所提升，却牺牲了可解释性且依赖标注数据和模型训练。大语言模型具备强大的推理能力和世界知识，为实现可解释、无标签的社区检测提供了新的可能。

Method: 受自然系统相变动力学的启发，提出了四阶段流程：(1) 局部视图社区初始化：利用k-ego上下文和无监督节点角色编码局部图结构；(2) 多因子社区合并：利用LLM诱导的规则迭代合并局部社区；(3) 多粒度社区精炼：并行应用LLM诱导的从粗到细的规则以减少边界噪声；(4) 全局视图社区选择：基于拓扑紧凑性和边界清晰度识别高质量社区。该方法将LLM的隐式知识转化为显式可解释的逻辑结构。

Result: 在真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，LUCID作为无监督方法达到了最先进的性能，并且持续超越领先的无监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUCID成功地将大语言模型的推理能力与社区检测任务相结合，实现了无需标注数据、免训练且具有良好可解释性的高性能社区检测，为图分析领域提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental task in graph analytics that aims to identify cohesive groups of entities with similar behaviors or interests. Classic objective-driven methods struggle with complex graph structures, while deep-learning approaches improve performance at the expense of interpretability and rely on labeled data and training. Large language models (LLMs), with strong reasoning capabilities and world knowledge, are promising for interpretable, label-free community detection. To leverage these strengths, we propose LUCID, an LLM-guided, interpretable, training-free, and unsupervised community detection method. Inspired by phase-transition kinetics in natural systems, where complex structures emerge through initialization, merging, refinement, and selection, LUCID is designed as a four-stage pipeline. Within this pipeline, the LLM induces formal rules that translate implicit knowledge into explicit and interpretable logical structures. Specifically, (1) the Local-View Community Initialization stage encodes local graph structures using k-ego contexts and unsupervised node roles; (2) the Multi-factor Community Merge stage uses LLM-induced rules to iteratively merge local communities; (3) the Multi-grain Community Refinement stage applies LLM-induced coarse-to-fine rules in parallel to reduce boundary noise; and (4) the Global-view Community Selection stage identifies high-quality communities based on topological compactness and boundary clarity. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that LUCID, as an unsupervised approach, achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently outperforms leading unsupervised and semi-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [29] [ADIAS: Automated Design of Interactive Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06410)
*Lekang Jiang,Bohan Tang,Stephan Goetz,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ADIAS框架，通过显式持久的议题状态引导智能体优化，在五个交互式基准测试上平均比最强基线提升25.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化智能体设计方法采用以候选者为中心的方式，跨轮次经验围绕候选智能体组织，导致修复进展隐含化，造成修复目标低效、部分进展整合缓慢、无效干预跨轮次传播等问题。

Method: 提出以议题为中心的智能体优化方法，并实例化为ADIAS框架，包含两个核心机制：1）持久议题状态维护稳定的议题身份、生命周期状态、支撑证据和干预-结果历史；2）议题引导优化利用该状态联合提出修复目标和修订方向，进行后续聚焦的全代码修改。

Result: 在五个交互式基准测试中，ADIAS平均比最强基线提升25.2%，在四个骨干模型上均获得一致提升；控制消融实验显示，移除持久议题状态或用以候选者为中心的策略替换以议题为中心的修订会导致高达40.7%的性能下降。

Conclusion: 以议题为中心的优化方法通过显式持久的议题状态有效引导优化过程，避免了以候选者为中心方法的固有缺陷，显著提升了自动化智能体设计的性能。

Abstract: Automated agent design improves agent harnesses through iterative revision, evaluation, and feedback summarization. Existing methods are largely candidate-centric: cross-round experience is organized around candidate agents, which leaves the repair progress implicit. This causes inefficient repair targeting, slow consolidation of partial progress, and propagation of ineffective interventions across rounds. Therefore, we formulate issue-centric agent optimization, in which repair progress is carried forward as an explicit persistent issue state to guide optimization, rather than re-derived from candidate history in each round. We instantiate the formulation in ADIAS, a framework for automated full-code agent design with two mechanisms. A persistent issue state maintains stable issue identities, lifecycle status, supporting evidence, and intervention-outcome histories. Issue-guided optimization uses this state to jointly propose repair targets and revision directions for subsequent focused full-code modification. Across five interactive benchmarks, ADIAS outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.2% on average and achieves consistent gains across four backbone models. Controlled ablations further show that removing persistent issue state or replacing issue-centric revision with candidate-centric policies leads to performance drops of up to 40.7%.

</details>


### [30] [Learning to Predict Middle-Layer Attention in MLLMs for Visual Token Prunin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06411)
*Yuyao Sun,Tao Deng,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Hao Geng,Minjun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAP方法，通过轻量级预测器在首个语言模型层之前预测视觉token重要性进行剪枝，在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上仅保留5.56%视觉token实现97.5%性能保持和3.09倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法使用预定义中间层的文本-视觉注意力进行指导，存在两个问题：(1) 对问题最响应的注意力层在不同样本间差异显著，固定层选择次优；(2) 获取合适中间层注意力需要大量视觉token经过多个语言模型层处理，计算成本已经很高。

Method: 提出Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP)方法：(1) 使用Question Contrastive Teacher Selection识别样本特定的教师层，通过对比原始问题和参考问题下的注意力进行选择；(2) 将选定层的注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器，该预测器从多模态输入特征估计视觉token重要性；(3) 推理时将预测的重要性分数与多样性准则结合，在首个语言模型层之前剪枝视觉token，无需注意力图且兼容现有推理加速技术。

Result: 在LLaVA-NeXT-7B的十个基准测试上，MAP仅使用5.56%的视觉token即可保留未剪枝模型97.5%的性能，实现3.09倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: MAP有效解决了现有视觉token剪枝方法的局限性，通过样本自适应的教师层选择和轻量级预测器实现了高效的视觉token剪枝，在保持性能的同时显著提升了多模态大语言模型的推理效率。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, but their efficiency is limited by the cost of processing numerous visual tokens. Visual token pruning can reduce this cost, but requires accurate token importance estimates. Recent studies have demonstrated that text-to-vision attention from middle language model layers can effectively guide visual token pruning, typically using attention from a predefined middle layer to select the visual tokens to retain. Two problems therefore remain. First, our analysis shows that the layer whose attention is most responsive to the question varies substantially across samples, making a fixed layer suboptimal. Second, obtaining attention from the appropriate middle layer requires processing numerous visual tokens through several language model layers, by which point considerable computation has already been spent. To address both problems, we propose Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP), which uses Question Contrastive Teacher Selection to identify a sample-specific teacher layer by contrasting attention under the original and reference questions, and distills attention from the selected layer into a lightweight predictor that estimates visual token importance from multi-modal input features. During inference, MAP combines the predicted importance scores with a diversity criterion to prune visual tokens before the first language model layer. Thus, MAP requires no attention maps for pruning and remains compatible with existing inference acceleration techniques. Across ten benchmarks on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, MAP retains 97.5% of the unpruned model performance with only 5.56% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.09x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [31] [WebGrader: Training LLMs for Web Development with Self-Evolving Programmatic Grader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06474)
*Boshui Chen,Huiping Liu,Shaolei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出WebGrader，一种自演进的程序化评分器，通过从网站请求中自动推导可执行的交互流程（Flow Contract）来为强化学习提供可靠的奖励信号，在WebGen-Bench上训练的8B模型达到52.01%功能成功率，超越o4-mini和DeepSeek-v4-flash。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型从自然语言描述生成完整网站时，强化学习训练受限于奖励设计瓶颈：手工编写的浏览器脚本成本高昂，而视觉语言模型(VLM)和GUI代理评分器可能在做最终判决前就过早给出结论，无法观察到决定性状态。

Method: WebGrader自主从网站请求中推导所需交互流程，表示为可执行的Flow Contract，在真实浏览器中实例化项目，将目标动作与源代码和实时DOM关联，沿浏览器轨迹收集视觉、DOM、响应和持久状态证据。通过残差驱动的离线循环发现可复用的验证器技能，在不相交的验证页面上筛选后冻结技能图谱用于策略训练。系统分离测试规划、动作定位、证据收集和语义判断。

Result: 在WebGen-Bench上，WebGrader训练的8B策略模型达到52.01%功能成功率，比匹配的外观加脚本奖励高出7.88个百分点，超越o4-mini和DeepSeek-v4-flash。在WG-core-250基准上，策略模型达到44.953满分，超越Qwen3-Coder-480B。

Conclusion: WebGrader通过程序化评分器将测试规划与执行验证解耦，确保仅在观察到请求的状态转换后才发出通过判定，有效解决了强化学习训练中的奖励设计瓶颈，显著提升了大语言模型网站生成的功能成功率。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete websites from natural-language descriptions, and reinforcement learning has become a central approach to closing their remaining functional gap. This training regime is bottlenecked by reward design. Hand-authored browser scripts are executable yet costly to write for open-ended requirements, while VLM and GUI-agent graders scale but may issue verdicts before observing the decisive state. We propose WebGrader, a self-evolving programmatic grader that autonomously derives the required interaction flows from each website request, represents each flow as an executable Flow Contract, and uses its execution outcome as an RL reward. WebGrader materializes the generated project in a live browser, grounds target actions against the source code and live DOM, and collects visual, DOM, response, and persistent-state evidence along the same browser trajectory. A residual-driven offline loop then discovers reusable verifier skills, screens them on disjoint validation pages, and freezes the promoted skill graph before policy training. By separating test planning, action grounding, evidence collection, and semantic judgment, WebGrader issues a Pass verdict only after observing the requested transition. On WebGen-Bench, WebGrader trains an 8B policy to a 52.01% functional success rate, outperforming a matched appearance-plus-script reward by 7.88 points and surpassing o4-mini and DeepSeek-v4-flash. On WG-core-250, the policy reaches a Full Score of 44.953 and surpasses Qwen3-Coder-480B.

</details>


### [32] [Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? Introducing C4 for Cross-Concept Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06501)
*Ming Wang,Yuqing Zhang,Tingna Xie,Xiangju Li,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了C4框架，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的跨概念创造力。通过成语跨概念编码解码任务，发现当前最强闭源模型准确率仅约50%，开源模型更低，揭示了MLLMs在创造性解码能力上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs的创造性能力在设计、通信、教育和人机协作中至关重要，但相比准确性导向的任务，由于缺乏明确目标和奖励信号，评估难度大。跨概念理解是接受性创造力的核心认知能力，能够使感知者从非显而易见但有意义的概念关系中恢复预期含义。现有评估方法难以有效衡量这种创造性能力。

Method: 提出C4框架，将项目构建建模为跨概念编码，将模型推理建模为跨概念解码。构建手动标注和第三方审核的跨概念网络，将目标槽映射到桥接路径上的可图像化替代概念。创建C4-Eval评估集，包含184个合成项目和37个人类创作的跨概念成语图形，每个项目在五种任务设置下实例化，产生884个主要答案恢复案例。评估了十个MLLMs。

Result: 在十个评估的MLLMs中，最强闭源模型达到50.7%和48.0%的主要准确率，开源模型明显更低。候选约束显著提高了准确率，但桥接提示和解释请求仅提供适度增益。结果表明当前MLLMs在通过跨概念关系解码创造性编码含义方面存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs在跨概念创造性解码能力上存在重大缺陷，即使在提供额外约束和提示的情况下，准确率仍然有限。C4框架为评估和促进MLLMs的创造性能力提供了系统化方法，揭示了未来研究需要重点突破的创造性解码瓶颈。

Abstract: Creative capabilities of MLLMs matter in design, communication, education, and human--AI collaboration, yet remain difficult to evaluate because explicit targets and reward signals are scarce compared with accuracy-oriented tasks. Cross-concept understanding is a core cognitive capacity underlying receptive creativity. It enables a perceiver to recover intended meaning from non-obvious but meaningful conceptual relations. We operationalize item construction as cross-concept encoding and model inference as cross-concept decoding. We introduce C4, a cognition-inspired evaluation framework for Chengyu (Chinese idiom)-based Cross-Concept Creativity. Its encoding component maps target slots to imageable substitute concepts along bridge paths in a manually annotated and third-party-reviewed cross-concept network, enabling batch generation with explicit structure, difficulty indexed by bridge count and depth, and exact answers. Using this framework, we instantiate the C4 Evaluation Set (C4-Eval), comprising 184 synthetic items and 37 human-created cross-concept chengyu figures collected from online sources. We manually construct and review cross-concept relations, bridge paths, and reasoning processes for the collected figures. Each C4-Eval item is instantiated in five task settings, yielding 884 primary answer-recovery cases. Across ten evaluated MLLMs, the strongest closed models reach 50.7% and 48.0% primary accuracy, while open-source models remain substantially lower. Candidate constraints improve accuracy sharply, but bridge hints and explanation requests provide only modest gains. These results expose a substantial gap in how current MLLMs decode creatively encoded meaning through cross-concept relations. The code is in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [33] [KNOWPLAN: Knowledge-Driven AI Agents for Smart Degree Pathway Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06530)
*Shuheng Cao,Weijia Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Xiyun Hu,Yat Yang,Juqy Chen,Zhaoxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KnowPlan系统，通过强制"先提取后规划"的分阶段架构，解决了从非结构化大学目录中自动规划学位路径的问题。在100所大学的测试中，实现了96.2%的目录召回率，100%硬可行性约束满足，99.5%的请求可认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从大学官方来源规划学位路径需要解决两个问题：首先从目录、网页、JSON端点和PDF等无统一schema的源中重建课程体系，然后优化学生特定路径。两者耦合会导致每种失败模式隐藏另一种：驱动自身爬取的规划器永远不会学到当前计划不需要的事实。因此需要分离提取和规划阶段，并测量阶段间接口而非假设其正确性。

Method: 提出两阶段系统KnowPlan：（1）CatalogBrowse：无用户profile访问的探索器，通过原子目录义务集合的期望边际增益评分合法动作，确定性解析并通过span约束的子句到AST模型回退，以索引、schema、溯源和引用完整性的闭包证书终止；输出三个溯源链接的JSON文档。（2）DegreeMap：仅消费上述文档，编译为类型化需求超图，使用CP-SAT在硬可行性、完成时限、负载、风险、个性化效用和期权价值上字典序优化，每阶段在前阶段的最优解内优化并保持可认证。

Result: 在100所大学广度轨道和六校深度轨道上：CatalogBrowse达96.2%库存召回率和88.7%掩码源恢复率，源访问量比全爬虫少47%；DegreeMap保持100.0%硬可行性，个性化效用比最强基线提升+0.066；完整管道认证99.5%的请求，与特权黄金图的效用差距为0.015。

Conclusion: 通过强制提取优先边界和测量阶段间接口，KnowPlan成功解耦了课程体系重建与学生路径优化，在保证可认证性和约束满足的同时，显著提升了效用和效率，为自动化学位规划提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Planning a degree from official university sources requires solving two problems in order. The institution's curriculum must first be reconstructed from catalogs, departmental pages, JSON endpoints, and PDFs that share no schema, and only then can a student-specific path be optimized under prerequisite logic and overlapping requirement constraints. Coupling the two lets each failure mode hide the other, because a planner that drives its own crawling never learns facts its current plan does not need. We present KnowPlan, which enforces an extraction-first boundary and measures the interface between the stages rather than assuming it. CatalogBrowse explores with no access to any user profile. It scores legal actions by lower-confidence expected marginal gain over a finite set of atomic catalog obligations per unit of source access, parses deterministically through platform adapters with a span-constrained clause-to-AST model fallback, and terminates on a closure certificate over index, schema, provenance, and reference completeness instead of a reward threshold. Its output contract is three provenance-linked JSON documents. DegreeMap consumes only those documents. It compiles them into a typed requirement hypergraph and optimizes lexicographically with CP-SAT over hard feasibility, completion horizon, load and risk, personalized utility, and option value, so that each stage optimizes inside the previous stage's proven optimum and stays certifiable within the solver budget. Across a 100-university broad track and a six-school dense track, CatalogBrowse reaches 96.2% inventory recall and 88.7% masked-source recovery at 47% less source access than an exhaustive crawler, DegreeMap holds 100.0% hard feasibility while improving personalized utility by +0.066 over the strongest baseline, and the full pipeline certifies 99.5% of requests with a utility gap to the privileged gold graph of 0.015.

</details>


### [34] [TaskSense: Focusing on What Matters in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06544)
*SM Mazharul Islam,Manfred Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TaskSense通过随机空间注意力机制和逆动力学辅助任务，使世界模型专注于任务相关区域，显著提升了在视觉干扰下的控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉控制世界模型通过重建观测来学习潜在状态，隐式地鼓励表示保留整个视觉输入的信息。然而，任务相关内容通常只占观测的一小部分，背景杂波和干扰物消耗了宝贵的表示容量。这种视觉重建与控制目标之间的不匹配导致潜在表示偏向于建模任务无关的视觉内容，稀释了控制相关特征的学习信号，在视觉干扰下严重降低了下游性能。

Method: 提出了TaskSense框架，通过基于前一潜在状态的可微分随机空间注意力机制，在潜在编码前强制任务相关性。引入逆动力学辅助目标引导注意力转向控制相关区域。世界模型仅重建被关注区域而非完整观测，鼓励潜在表示保留任务相关信息并丢弃无关视觉内容。解码器进一步以采样的注意力图为条件，确保随机注意力下的一致重建。

Result: 与DreamerV3基线相比，TaskSense在DeepMind Control Suite上保持竞争性能，在Distracting Control Suite上持续优于DreamerV3，展示了对视觉干扰的显著增强鲁棒性。定性分析确认，由逆动力学监督引导的学习注意力能够一致地定位控制相关区域，同时抑制无关视觉内容。

Conclusion: TaskSense成功解决了视觉重建与控制目标之间的不匹配问题，通过任务中心的注意力机制显著提升了世界模型在视觉干扰下的鲁棒性和性能，为视觉控制提供了一种更有效的表示学习范式。

Abstract: World models for visual control typically learn compact latent states by reconstructing observations, implicitly encouraging representations to preserve information across the entire visual input. However, task-relevant content often occupies only a small fraction of the observation, while background clutter and distractors consume valuable representational capacity. This mismatch between visual reconstruction and control objectives biases latent representations to model task-irrelevant visual content, diluting learning signals for control-relevant features and severely degrading downstream performance under visual distractions. We introduce TaskSense, a task-centric world modeling framework that enforces task relevance before latent encoding through a differentiable stochastic spatial attention mechanism conditioned on the previous latent state. To steer attention toward control-relevant regions, we augment training with an auxiliary inverse-dynamics objective. Rather than reconstructing the full observation, the world model reconstructs only the attended regions, encouraging latent representations to preserve task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant visual content.
